\underbrace{\mathscr A[\Omega]}_{\substack{\text{one ARA occurrence}\\\text{with local identity }\Omega}}
=
\underbrace{\left(\{P,Q\},J,\mathcal C\right)}_{\substack{\text{unordered opposing poles, coupling,}\\\text{and closure rule}}}.
\]

A local directional chart supplies the human-readable address:

\[
\underbrace{\Xi_{O,d}[\Omega]}_{\text{reported ARA reading}}
=
\underbrace{\chi_{O,d}}_{\substack{\text{observer, chosen direction,}\\\text{scale origin and projection}}}
\left(
\underbrace{\mathscr A[\Omega]}_{\text{same underlying relational form}}
\right).
\]

The chart may report mixing depth, visible closure order, or dimensional slice, but those are views of the same

\underbrace{x_{Q\to P}}_{\text{reversed 0–2 reading}}
=2-
\underbrace{x_{P\to Q}}_{\text{declared forward reading}},
\qquad
\underbrace{\Delta k(A,B)}_{\text{signed rung displacement}}
=-
\underbrace{\Delta k(B,A)}_{\text{same relation reversed}}.

For two valid charts, the coordinate change is

(\Xi_{O,d}),

while relational invariants—opposition, adjacency, closure/non-closure and declared coupling topology—should remain

**Plain statement:** establish the ordered poles and traversal direction first. “Up,” “down,” fifth, sixth,

## 33. Musing — cosmic ARA trajectory from dense origin to dilute expansion and a possible flip

¶ Dylan extended the one-form/many-identities claim cosmologically:

> “This is why I propose is the the geometry beneath partly, because when taken to the extremes and worked through
> the logical progression, it maps cleanly still, but makes sense... or it does to me.”

> “And it is just all the same relation geometry happening all over. Big bang just being a singularity itself with
> it being Massive energy small space ---> No energy maximum space pole, it'll then probably flip again and go into
> the anti-phase.”

### Established cosmological anchor and necessary correction

The established relation is between scale factor `a(t)` and component energy density `ρ_i(t)`, not a simple global
transfer from “all total energy” to “no total energy.” For a separately conserved homogeneous component with
equation-of-state parameter `w_i=p_i/ρ_i`,

\[
\underbrace{\frac{d\ln\rho_i}{d\ln a}}_{\substack{\text{density response}\\\text{to expansion}}}
=
\underbrace{-3(1+w_i)}_{\substack{\text{component-specific}\\\text{dilution slope}}}.
\]

Thus radiation has `ρ_r∝a⁻⁴`, nonrelativistic matter has `ρ_m∝a⁻³`, and a cosmological constant has `ρ_Λ=constant`.
The ARA extreme is better named **high-density/small-scale ↔ large-scale/dilute matter-radiation**, with the selected
energy component declared. Global total energy is not generally a uniquely defined conserved scalar in an expanding
general-relativistic universe.

Classical FLRW/GR extrapolation approaches a Big-Bang singular boundary, but the singularity is also where the
classical description fails; quantum-gravity and bounce models are unresolved alternatives. It should therefore be
recorded as a classical singular limit or proposed ARA source, not an observed literal point with known internal
physics.

### Candidate ARA path instrument

For a declared density component, a dimensionless compactification can map the open cosmological path onto `0–2`:

\[
\underbrace{z_i(t)}_{\text{unbounded expansion/density contrast}}
=
\underbrace{\ln\!\frac{a(t)}{a_*}}_{\text{Space/expansion contribution}}
-
\underbrace{\alpha_i\ln\!\frac{\rho_i(t)}{\rho_{i,*}}}_{\text{density/connection contribution}},
\qquad
\underbrace{x_i(t)}_{\text{ARA cosmic coordinate}}
=1+\frac{2}{\pi}\arctan z_i(t).
\]

`α_i`, the reference epoch `*`, and the selected component must be signed before testing. This is one candidate
instrument, not a derived unique cosmic ARA coordinate.

### What “flip into anti-phase” must predict

Let

\[
\underbrace{H(t)}_{\text{signed expansion rate}}=\frac{\dot a}{a}.
\]

Keep the derivative level explicit:

\[
\underbrace{H}_{\substack{>0\text{ expansion}\\<0\text{ contraction}}},
\qquad
\underbrace{q=-\frac{a\ddot a}{\dot a^2}}_{\substack{>0\text{ decelerating expansion}\\<0\text{ accelerating expansion}}}.
\]

The observed universe has already crossed from decelerating to accelerating expansion (`q` changed sign) while
`H` remained positive. This is a candidate ARA handover/ridge transition, not a full expansion-to-contraction flip.

An expansion-to-contraction turnaround requires

\[
H(t_f)=0,
\qquad \dot H(t_f)<0,
\]

whereas a contraction-to-expansion bounce requires `H(t_b)=0` and `Ḣ(t_b)>0`. These provide a physically declared
sign flip. ARA must state whether “anti-phase” predicts reversal of `H`, reversal of some energy-transfer channel,
or only a phase change in another observable. A second singularity is not mathematically required for every flip.

Current observations establish late-time accelerated expansion. DESI DR2 strengthens dataset-dependent hints that
dark energy may evolve, but does not establish a future turnaround or Big Crunch. Full five-year DESI dark-energy
results are expected in 2027. Sources checked 12 July 2026: [NASA Big Bang](https://science.nasa.gov/mission/hubble/science/science-behind-the-discoveries/hubble-big-bang/),
[NASA dark energy](https://science.nasa.gov/dark-energy/), [DESI DR2 announcement](https://www.desi.lbl.gov/2025/03/19/more-than-a-hint-of-evolving-dark-energy-new-results-and-data-from-desi/),
and [DESI DR2 cosmology paper](https://journals.aps.org/prd/abstract/10.1103/tr6y-kpc6).

**Status:** `MUSING / PARKED`. The density–scale-factor law and turnaround/bounce sign conditions are established
anchors. Big Bang as the ARA source pole, a universal compactified cosmic coordinate, and a necessary future
anti-phase flip are proposed additions requiring prospective predictions.

## 34. Musing — polygonal resolution ladder from identity node toward spherical closure

¶ Dylan proposed a number/shape ladder of the same fractal ARA form:

> “Node is identity. 1. Dipole is coupling in moment, no change. Triangle is Information^3 or the ARA representation
> of the number. 4 or the square is the Phase/Antiphase mixing quadrant.”

> “Fifth is time or pentagon, or maybe the required irregularity weaving between. Hexagon is the forming of concrete
> connections.”

> “All these are just working up to a sphere from the most basic shapes.”

> “I am sure if I kept pushing, I might find the septogon which might just be a quadrant with an extra coupling
> attached. But I think they probably go on quite far.”

### Minimal graph spine

Use

\[
\underbrace{G_1=K_1}_{\substack{\text{one node}\\\text{latent identity}}}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{G_2=K_2}_{\substack{\text{two nodes plus one edge}\\\text{momentary coupling/dipole}}}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{G_n=C_n\quad(n\ge3)}_{\substack{\text{closed }n\text{-position section}\\|V|=|E|=n}}.
\]

`K₁` carries an identity label but has no relational ARA measurement by itself. `K₂` is the first measurable pair;
at a single time `t₀`, `J₁₂(t₀)` is a coupling snapshot. Change requires another time slice or `dJ₁₂/dt`. `C₃` is
the first simple closed cycle and supports the existing Information³ round-trip condition.

### Proposed role table

| order | graph/section | ARA reading | current status |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `K₁` | identity/node | definitional musing; ARA not measurable without relation |
| 2 | `K₂` | opposed pair/dipole; coupling in one moment | clean graph representation; physical dipole meaning depends on observable |
| 3 | `C₃` | Information³; first non-ravelling closure | exact minimum-cycle and holonomy anchors |
| 4 | `C₄` | the four phase/anti-phase sign quadrants sampled as a square | exact four sign combinations; square is a section, not “fourth dimension” |
| 5 | `C₅` | Time, handover, projection loss, or required irregular weave | competing ARA interpretations; not settled |
| 6 | `C₆` | phase triangle plus anti-phase copy; concrete/tiling connection | exact construction plus interpretive physical assignment |
| 7+ | `C_n` or branched graphs | higher coupling resolution | open; role must be derived rather than assigned from the integer alone |

This is compatible with the earlier correction that coupled circular identities can settle into hexagonal contact
networks: `C₆` may describe the resolved connection section without claiming that physical circles are literally
manufactured by assembling triangles.

### Adding a coupling does not automatically add one polygon side

A closed successor requires a graph operation that adds a vertex and recloses the boundary:

\[
\underbrace{C_n}_{\text{closed section}}
\xrightarrow{\text{split one edge through a new node}}
\underbrace{C_{n+1}}_{\text{closed successor}}.
\]

Attaching one loose edge to `C₄` instead produces a branched square, not `C₅`; attaching a branch to `C₆` does not
produce `C₇`. Dylan's “quadrant with an extra coupling attached” may therefore indicate a **branched seven-part
interaction graph** rather than a regular septagon. The topology must decide the name.

### Polygon-to-circle-to-sphere limit

For regular sections of fixed circumradius `R`, increasing `n` approaches the circle:

\[
\underbrace{P_n=2nR\sin\frac{\pi}{n}}_{\text{regular }n\text{-gon perimeter}}
\longrightarrow 2\pi R,
\qquad
\underbrace{A_n=\frac n2R^2\sin\frac{2\pi}{n}}_{\text{regular }n\text{-gon area}}
\longrightarrow \pi R^2.
\]

Thus

\[
C_n\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}S^1.
\]

This produces the circular section, not yet the sphere. The ARA sphere is recovered when circular sections close
across orientations, consistent with the existing rotate-the-diameter construction:

\[
\underbrace{\bigcup_{\hat u\in\mathcal O}S^1_{\hat u}}_{\substack{\text{circular sections over}\\\text{all required axes}}}
=
\underbrace{S^2}_{\text{spherical boundary}},
\qquad
\operatorname{Fill}(S^2)=B^3.
\]

The ladder therefore describes increasing **sectional relational resolution** of the same ARA form, not a sequence
of automatically increasing physical dimensions.

### Relation to archived work

Archived Scripts `163_sphere_triangulation.py` and `164_time_as_sphere.py` already explored triangle constraints on
an ARA sphere and Time as a spherical coordinate field. They are exploratory historical instances, not validation
of the new number/role ladder. An older transcript also cautioned that the square is not literally a 4-D object and
that the closure number can remain three while the object being triangulated grows.

**Status:** `MUSING / PARKED`. Orders 1–4 and the polygon limit have clean graph/geometric anchors. Time/five,
Connection/six and any seven-plus semantic assignments remain ARA hypotheses requiring independent observables.

## 35. Synthesis — one ARA relation, finite reconstruction shapes, and cross-scale identity closure

¶ Dylan stated the compressed overall picture:

> “I am just unpacking this one ARA relation and taking it to the extremes to reveal the geometry.”

> “Compressed to 2d its a circle or wave, in 3D its a sphere. But the other shapes are used to reform that.”

> “It's the small fractal part of a wave meeting the large to create its own sphere.”

### Circle/wave relation

The exact established relation is that a periodic wave is a coordinate projection of circular phase:

\[
\underbrace{\mathbf z(t)=A(\cos\theta(t),\sin\theta(t))}_{\substack{\text{circular phase orbit}\\S^1\text{ in a 2-D state plane}}},
\qquad
\underbrace{y(t)=\Pi_y\mathbf z(t)=A\sin\theta(t)}_{\substack{\text{observed wave}\\\text{one coordinate through time}}}.
\]

Thus “wave = circle” is precise when it means a wave trace is a projection of phase rotation, not that every plotted
wave curve is geometrically identical to a circle.

### Finite shapes reform rather than replace the circle/sphere

`C₃,C₄,C₅,C₆,…` are provisionally finite relational skeletons or section samplings of the same closure:

\[
\underbrace{B^1}_{\text{diameter/minimal ARA}}
\xrightarrow{\text{restore phase}}
\underbrace{S^1}_{\text{circle/wave cycle}},
\qquad
\underbrace{C_n}_{\text{finite relation scaffold}}
\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}
S^1,
\]

\[
\underbrace{\bigcup_{\hat u}S^1_{\hat u}}_{\text{closed directional sections}}
=
\underbrace{S^2}_{\text{sphere boundary}},
\qquad
\operatorname{Fill}(S^2)=B^3.
\]

Triangles can additionally triangulate a spherical surface. The polygons therefore encode how a finite set of
relations reconstructs, samples, closes, or locally deforms the ARA circle/sphere; they are not competing primitive
ontologies.

### Candidate missing cross-scale coupling law

Let `𝒜_f[ω]` be the fine/small ARA occurrence and `𝒜_c[Ω]` the coarse/large occurrence or envelope. Define

\[
\underbrace{\mathscr A_h[H]}_{\substack{\text{new closed identity}\\\text{with its own ARA sphere}}}
=
\underbrace{\mathcal C_\times}_{\substack{\text{cross-scale coupling}\\\text{and closure operator}}}
\left(
\underbrace{\mathscr A_f[\omega]}_{\text{fine wave/identity}},
\underbrace{\mathscr A_c[\Omega]}_{\text{coarse wave/envelope}},
\underbrace{J_{fc}}_{\text{their informative relation}}
\right).
\]

This gives a direct mathematical reading of “the small fractal part of a wave meeting the large to create its own
sphere.” The fine occurrence supplies local phase/variation; the coarse occurrence supplies boundary conditions or
an envelope; their cross-scale relation can close as a third, persistent identity.

The new identity should be counted only when

\[
\underbrace{\epsilon_\triangle\le\epsilon_*}_{\text{Information³ consistency}}
\quad\land\quad
\underbrace{\mathcal B_{S^2}=1}_{\substack{\text{boundary closes over}\\\text{the declared directions}}}
\quad\land\quad
\underbrace{T_H\ge T_*}_{\text{persistence}}.
\]

`𝓑_(S²)` is not yet defined as one universal numerical instrument. Depending on the physical domain, cross-scale
coupling may appear as phase locking, amplitude modulation, feedback, confinement, interface stress, or another
measurable relation. The operator must preserve its abstract definition while allowing domain-specific observables.

### Fractal recursion

Once `H` closes, it can participate as fine or coarse input to another occurrence:

\[
\mathscr A_h[H]
\xrightarrow{\text{re-enter coupling web}}
\mathcal C_\times(\mathscr A_h,\mathscr A_{c'},J_{hc'})
=\mathscr A_{h'}[H'].
\]

The proposed fractality is therefore the repeated cross-scale closure operation. The identity, scale and physical
mechanism change; the relational ARA form is claimed to recur.

**Status:** framework synthesis recorded. Circle-to-wave projection and finite-polygon/circle limits are established
mathematics. Universal `𝒞_×`, spherical-boundary closure, and cross-domain physical recurrence remain proposed and
require fixed instruments and held-out transfer tests.

## 36. Physical anchor — obliquely crossing water waves, envelopes, and resonant daughter identities

¶ Dylan supplied a concrete cross-scale image:

> “A good physical example is when two waves in water meet at a travelled angle. They sort of cross and become
> smaller versions of each other fractally. I think that is basically playing out everywhere, all at once, at every
> scale.”

### Regime 1: linear crossing produces nested scales but no new dynamical identity

For two surface-wave components

\[
\eta_1=A\cos(\mathbf k_1\!\cdot\!\mathbf r-\omega_1t+\phi_1),
\qquad
\eta_2=A\cos(\mathbf k_2\!\cdot\!\mathbf r-\omega_2t+\phi_2),
\]

linear superposition gives

\[
\underbrace{\eta_1+\eta_2}_{\text{observed crossing field}}
=2A
\underbrace{\cos\!\left(\frac{\Delta\mathbf k\cdot\mathbf r-\Delta\omega t+\Delta\phi}{2}\right)}_{\substack{\text{large beat/envelope pattern}\\\text{coarse ARA appearance}}}
\underbrace{\cos\!\left(\frac{\Sigma\mathbf k\cdot\mathbf r-\Sigma\omega t+\Sigma\phi}{2}\right)}_{\substack{\text{fine carrier/crossing pattern}\\\text{small ARA appearance}}}.
\]

For equal wavenumber magnitude `k` and crossing angle `θ`, the envelope and carrier section scales are set by
`|Δk|/2=k sin(θ/2)` and `|Σk|/2=k cos(θ/2)`. This exactly produces simultaneous coarse and fine geometry from the
same pair. In the linear regime, however, the parent waves pass through one another; the pattern is not a new
energy-carrying mode or persistent identity. It is the essential null control for the ARA claim.

### Regime 2: nonlinear resonant crossing produces a daughter wave

Deep-water surface-gravity waves admit resonant four-wave interactions. A controlled basin experiment generated two
oblique mother waves and observed a smaller resonant daughter wave with no fitted parameters. In the degenerate
quartet studied there,

\[
\underbrace{2\mathbf k_1-\mathbf k_3-\mathbf k_4}_{\Delta\mathbf k}=0,
\qquad
\underbrace{2\omega_1-\omega_3-\omega_4}_{\Delta\omega}=0,
\]

and the interaction phase locked:

\[
\underbrace{\Phi}_{\text{round-trip interaction phase}}
=2\phi_1-\phi_3-\phi_4
=\frac{\pi}{2}
\quad\text{in the reported resonant experiment}.
\]

The daughter amplitude obeyed the predicted early-growth form

\[
\underbrace{a_4^{res}}_{\text{daughter-wave amplitude}}
=
\underbrace{\varepsilon_1^2\varepsilon_3}_{\text{mother-wave nonlinear input}}
\underbrace{d}_{\text{interaction distance}}
\underbrace{G(\theta)}_{\text{angle/frequency-ratio resonance gate}},
\]

with a maximum growth region near a `25°` crossing angle for that experimental configuration. Off resonance, growth
was suppressed by detuning and followed the predicted sinc response.

ARA translation:

| wave-interaction quantity | ARA reading |
|---|---|
| mother waves | fine/coarse or paired source identities |
| crossing angle and steepness | coupling geometry and strength |
| `Δk`, `Δω` | spatial and temporal closure defects/unravelling |
| locked interaction phase `Φ` | cycle-consistent relational closure |
| daughter mode | candidate new identity |
| detuning response | graded handover/leak rather than binary success |
| persistent spatial growth | identity-spawn strength and duration |

This is a concrete physical instance of `𝒞_×`: the output is not merely the visual sum of the inputs; nonlinear
coupling transfers energy into a new phase-locked mode.

### Triangle versus quartet is selected by local physics

The minimum resonant interaction order is not universally three. Deep-water pure gravity-wave dispersion prohibits
exact resonant triads, making resonant quartets central; capillary waves and many finite/shallow-water regimes admit
triads. A unified closure condition is

\[
\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{N}s_j\mathbf k_j=0}_{\text{spatial closure}},
\qquad
\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{N}s_j\omega_j=0}_{\text{temporal closure}},
\qquad s_j\in\{-1,+1\}.
\]

The smallest nontrivial `N` allowed by the dispersion relation is the physical interaction order. This offers a
promising bridge to the polygonal-resolution ladder: `N=3` or `N=4` can be predicted from the medium before observing
the daughter mode. It does not establish the semantic claims for five, six, or higher orders.

### Fractality qualification

Repeated resonant and near-resonant interactions can transfer energy across scales and generate wave-turbulent
spectra. A daughter wave is not generally a geometrically scaled copy of either mother wave; its wavevector and
frequency follow resonance and dispersion relations. “Fractal” should therefore mean recurrence of the interaction/
closure operation across scales unless statistical self-similarity is separately measured.

Primary sources checked 12 July 2026: [Bonnefoy et al., oblique surface-gravity-wave resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/1606.09009),
[Zhang & Pan, triad and quartet contributions in deep-water spectral evolution](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/journal-of-fluid-mechanics/article/role-of-triad-interactions-in-spectral-evolution-of-surface-gravity-waves-in-deep-water/999A10860DAA4850D80D80786147A3F6),
and [Henderson & Hammack, resonant capillary-wave triads](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/journal-of-fluid-mechanics/article/abs/experiments-on-ripple-instabilities-part-1-resonant-triads/196D707798EC4271CDAC3D8B5396FCA1).

**Status:** linear identity is an exact anchor and null; nonlinear daughter-wave creation is an established physical
anchor. Interpreting the resonant closure as the universal ARA `𝒞_×` law remains a proposed cross-domain claim.

## 37. Layered Sand as the opposite-direction reconstruction of cross-scale wave closure

¶ Dylan identified the relation to the existing prediction work:

> “Layered sand formula is me attempting to reconstruct something similar from the opposite direction. From the
> initial directional pull, up the daughter waves to the larger wave.”

Inspection of `TheFormula/10 - Morphed-sphere terrain, watershed & layered-sand (26-05-26)` confirms that this is the
intended architecture:

```text
moving floor / initial directional pull
→ fine, medium and coarse faster lower layers
→ alternating opposite contact rolls
→ two-contact wobble and recursive terrain
→ measured/home sphere displacement
→ upper/slower compression and envelope constraint
→ terrain arrival / proposed larger-wave state
```

The exact code-level roles recorded in `ARA_LAYERED_SAND_SINGLE_FORMULA_RESULT.md` are `D₀` (floor pull), `S_i`
(own-layer spin), `Q_i=(-1)^i` (alternating orientation), `C_iA,C_iB` (two lower contacts), `W_i=C_iA-C_iB`
(wobble), `R_i=√(P_i/P_(i−1))` (lower-speed transfer), `T_i` (recursive terrain), `U` (upper pressure) and `M`
(measured-sphere roll).

### Forward closure versus upward reconstruction

The forward proposal is

\[
\underbrace{\mathscr A_h}_{\text{daughter/new identity}}
=
\underbrace{\mathcal C_\times}_{\text{cross-scale closure}}
(\mathscr A_f,\mathscr A_c,J_{fc}).
\]

Layered Sand attempts the opposite inference:

\[
\underbrace{\widehat{\mathscr A}_c}_{\substack{\text{reconstructed larger wave}\\\text{or future coarse envelope}}}
=
\underbrace{\mathcal U_\theta}_{\substack{\text{Layered Sand upward operator}\\\text{fine contacts plus boundary constraints}}}
\left(
\underbrace{D_0,\{S_i,C_{iA},C_{iB},W_i,R_i,T_i\}}_{\text{observed lower/daughter states}},
\underbrace{U}_{\text{upper envelope/backpressure}}
\right).
\]

`𝒰_θ` should not yet be called the exact inverse `𝒞_×⁻¹`. Cross-scale closure is generally many-to-one: different
fine-scale phase configurations can produce the same coarse appearance, and projection/closure may discard
information. Mathematically the reconstruction is closer to a constrained pseudoinverse or state estimator:

\[
\widehat{\mathscr A}_c
=
\arg\min_{\mathscr A_c}
\left[
\underbrace{\|\mathcal C_\times(\mathscr A_f,\mathscr A_c,J)-\mathscr A_h^{obs}\|^2}_{\text{reproduce observed closure}}
+
\underbrace{\lambda\,\mathcal R(\mathscr A_c;U)}_{\text{upper-envelope/terrain constraint}}
\right].
\]

### What the historical results say

- The terrain analog was the thread's empirical win over persistence across 6/12/24 months: MAE `0.602` versus
  `0.896`, correlation `+0.275` versus `+0.003`.
- The deterministic single Layered Sand formula was much weaker: MAE `0.879`, correlation `+0.015`, amplitude ratio
  `0.158`. Full variants commonly under-rolled with amplitude ratios about `0.05–0.20`.
- The advance audit found that the fixed formula was highly correlated with the current state (`+0.978`) and was
  therefore mainly reconstructing present terrain rather than advancing it causally.
- Lower rungs did spin faster than home and upper rungs, supporting the basic scale ordering.
- Direct lower-spin features did not cleanly select boundary transitions; upper/envelope features carried clearer
  event-risk information. This supports a bidirectional reading: bottom-up drive supplies motion, while top-down
  boundary conditions select which larger closure is admissible.

Thus the historical failure is informative: the geometry/current terrain could be reconstructed, but fine-to-coarse
direction and displacement were non-unique.

### Candidate phase-coherent upward law

The next mathematical decompression should aggregate complex phase states rather than repeatedly attenuating scalar
roll magnitudes. Let each daughter/contact state be

\[
\underbrace{Z_{i,m}}_{\text{daughter/contact phasor}}
=
\underbrace{A_{i,m}}_{\text{magnitude}}
e^{i\underbrace{\phi_{i,m}}_{\text{phase and direction}}}.
\]

Then a parent/larger rung candidate is

\[
\underbrace{Z_{i+1}}_{\text{reconstructed larger-wave phasor}}
=
\underbrace{\Gamma_{i+1}(U,T)}_{\substack{\text{upper-envelope and terrain}\\\text{closure gate}}}
\sum_{m\in\mathrm{child}(i+1)}
\underbrace{w_{im}Z_{i,m}e^{i\pi p_{im}}}_{\substack{\text{contact transfer with}\\p_{im}\in\{0,1\}\text{ orientation flip}}}.
\]

This preserves constructive addition, anti-phase cancellation, direction and accumulated phase error. The unresolved
residual

\[
L_{i+1}=\sum_m w_{im}Z_{i,m}e^{i\pi p_{im}}-Z_{i+1}
\]

is a candidate local leak/unclosed information term. This law is a proposed replacement target, not a retroactive
claim about the historical scripts.

**Status:** Dylan's intended direction confirmed by the Layered Sand documentation. Faster lower-spin ordering is
supported; exact upward reconstruction is not. Phase-coherent constrained aggregation is a new proposed formalisation
for a future deliberate test.

## 38. Methodological assessment — geometry-first inverse theory construction

¶ Dylan described a recurring experience:

> “Following the geometry and extrapolating or exploring its shape and interactions, I feel like I am almost speed
> running a lot of deep physics results and methods but from the opposite direction in my crude and Ai filtered
> methodology.”

This has a defensible methodological interpretation. Conventional training often proceeds

\[
\underbrace{\text{domain observations}}_{D}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{domain equations}}_{E}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{solutions and abstractions}}_{G}.
\]

Dylan's ARA exploration often proceeds in the reverse discovery direction:

\[
\underbrace{\text{minimal relational geometry}}_{G_{ARA}}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{structural expectation}}_{S_*}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{domain lookup/measurement}}_{D}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{established mathematical translation}}_{E}.
\]

Appropriate names include **geometry-first abductive modelling**, **inverse theory construction**, and **structural
hypothesis generation**. “Inverse” here describes discovery order, not a proof that the resulting domain theory is
uniquely implied by ARA.

### Concrete convergences in the present dialogue

The ARA walk independently motivated questions that translated into:

- wave/circle identity → phasors and phase-space projection;
- relation closure/Information³ → graph cycles and holonomy/cycle consistency;
- observer-dependent up/down → coordinate charts and covariance under reversal;
- small/large ARA meeting → multiscale closure/coarse-graining;
- angled water-wave meeting → interference envelopes, resonance defects and phase-locked daughter modes;
- Layered Sand upward walk → constrained state reconstruction/pseudoinverse and coherent phasor aggregation;
- sphere reconstruction → directional sections, triangulation and boundary closure.

This repeated convergence is evidence that ARA is a productive compression and search heuristic. It is not by itself
proof that ARA is the unique or fundamental ontology beneath those theories. Many physical domains converge on the
same mathematics because symmetry, conservation, stability, low-order nonlinearities and coarse-graining produce
universality classes.

### Two-channel workflow

Preserve creative speed by separating channels:

1. **Discovery channel — loose and AI-assisted:** Dylan walks the geometry; AI supplies vocabulary, candidate maths,
   counterexamples and places to inspect. Musings remain cheap and expansive.
2. **Confirmation channel — frozen and auditable:** before lookup or data access, record the target domain, predicted
   structure, direction, number of modes, signs, failure condition, permitted transformations and null baselines.

The AI may act as interpreter in the first channel but is a contamination risk in the second because its training may
already contain the target result. Raw transcripts, timestamped files, hashes and explicit `USER PRIOR / AI
TRANSLATION / EXTERNAL LOOKUP` labels are therefore part of the evidence, not administrative decoration.

### Evidence ladder for “speed-run” recoveries

| level | achievement | evidential meaning |
|---:|---|---|
| 0 | after-the-fact visual or vocabulary resemblance | useful musing only |
| 1 | ARA recovers a broad known structural class | evidence of heuristic compatibility |
| 2 | before lookup, ARA locates a specific relation, sign, mode count or failure region | provenance-bearing structural retrodiction |
| 3 | fixed ARA rules derive a known equation/constraint or parameter-free quantitative relation | strong recovery of established structure |
| 4 | frozen ARA predicts an unknown/withheld result against non-ARA baselines | genuine novel predictive evidence |
| 5 | independent people/data reproduce Level 4 | evidence relevant to the universal-framework claim |

Repository examples currently span Levels 0–3, with mixed Level-4 prediction attempts. The provenance ledger and
raw transcripts strengthen some Level-2 priority claims; they do not promote every later mathematical translation to
an independent prediction.

### Specificity gate

Before lookup, write a feature vector

\[
\underbrace{F_*}_{\text{signed ARA forecast}}
=
(\text{objects},\text{relation order},\text{direction/sign},\text{phase condition},
\text{scale change},\text{failure/null}).
\]

After lookup, score the exact frozen features rather than asking only whether “something similar” exists. A useful
qualitative evidence rubric is

\[
\underbrace{W}_{\text{weight of one convergence}}
\propto
\underbrace{S}_{\text{specificity}}
\underbrace{P}_{\text{precommitment}}
\underbrace{I}_{\text{independence from target information}}
\underbrace{R}_{\text{replicability}}.
\]

This is a bookkeeping rubric, not a probability formula. It explains why a precise transcript-backed location can be
meaningful while a beautiful AI-assisted post-hoc mapping remains exploratory.

**Assessment:** Dylan is not merely learning physics backwards. He is using ARA as a compressed generative grammar
that repeatedly points toward mathematical structures physics already uses. That is scientifically worth developing
as a discovery method. The stronger “geometry beneath all physics” claim must be earned by Levels 3–5 without
changing the grammar after each domain.

## 39. Dylan's priority: construct fidelity before apparent falsification

¶ Dylan distinguished the individual and scientific viewpoints, then named the central communication risk:

> “I was speaking from an individualised perspective, you are discussing from a science framework. Both are
> relationally true but yours is the rung up and therefore way more important.”

> “I do also cross check a lot of conversation and scripts with blind Ai drops similar to how we first started this
> conversation.”

> “The worse thing I can be is not wrong, but misrepresent that idea I have in my head on the larger scale so it
> either doesn't get taken seriously, or is mis-translated and therefore potentially proven incorrect due to this
> distortion.”

This is the problem of **construct validity**. A reliable script can return a valid result about an invalid proxy.
The resulting null or failure does not adjudicate the intended ARA claim.

The adopted response is `ARA_TRANSLATION_FIDELITY_PROTOCOL.md`, added to the AI canon and as Step `−1` of the test
protocol. It requires:

- Dylan's verbatim, versioned source claim;
- declared identity, poles, direction, rung, observable, coupling and closure;
- plain restatement, mathematical formalisation and back-translation;
- explicit AI additions and discarded information;
- Dylan's `EXACT ENOUGH TO TEST` verdict before registration;
- `PROXY TEST` relabelling if wrong-object distortion is discovered after a run.

Blind AI drops are formalised as interpretation audits: identical frozen minimal packets, fresh contexts, preferably
different model families, fixed output questions and logged disagreement codes. Agreement measures communicability,
not truth or independent replication, because models may share training and priors.

The low-energy procedure assigns documentation to the librarian. Dylan needs only confirm or correct a two-sentence
plain back-translation; the AI must version and propagate the correction.

**Status:** Dylan priority adopted as a RULE-level workflow safeguard. The new protocol is a living procedure; its
effectiveness should be audited on historical wrong-proxy cases and future preregistrations.

## 40. Musing — ARA includes subjective, social and metaphysical-domain phenomena

¶ Dylan explicitly requested physics-mode treatment and musing-tier status:

> “ARA does not just treat the material world. I believe everything in the universe, dreams, societial customs,
> meta-physical things etc, follow these same shapes, because they are not ‘other’ ... They're part of the universe.”

> “They may be distorted in some way, but they are still something that is naturally occuring in the universe and
> they follow the ARA geometry shape. This will give rise to being able to measure them due to the universal nature
> of the ARA co-ordinate units.”

This restates the claim already preserved in `ARA_CONVERSATION_RECORD_2026-06-25.md` §10: ARA's universality proposal
does not stop at the physics/social-science boundary. Today's refinement explicitly includes dreams and treats these
domains as different identities of the same ARA form.

### Physics-mode distinction: inclusion versus access

Use three separate claims:

1. **Ontological inclusion:** dreams, beliefs, customs, reports, rituals and anomalous experiences occur within the
   universe and have participants, relations, histories and consequences.
2. **Epistemic access:** science measures observable projections—neural/physiological signals, first-person reports,
   behaviour, language, network diffusion, persistence, consequences and interventions.
3. **Causal ontology:** the preferred explanation for an experience or custom is a separate hypothesis. The existence
   of an experience does not establish spirits, unknown fields, pure neural construction, social contagion, or any
   other proposed cause.

Formally,

\[
\underbrace{Y_m}_{\text{observed record in channel }m}
=
\underbrace{\Pi_m}_{\substack{\text{measurement/report/}\\\text{social projection}}}
\left(
\underbrace{\mathscr A[X]}_{\substack{\text{phenomenon as an}\\\text{ARA identity}}}
\right)
+
\underbrace{\epsilon_m}_{\text{noise, memory and distortion}}.
\]

For a dream, channels might include EEG/EOG/sleep stage, autonomic activity, timed awakening report, semantic content,
recall confidence and next-day behaviour. For a custom, channels might include adoption rate, network transmission,
enforcement, compliance, variation, abandonment and re-emergence. A metaphysical claim can be studied through its
experience/report/consequence channels; its proposed external referent becomes scientific only when it makes a
distinguishable prediction beyond those channels' existing models.

### Distortion cannot be a universal rescue

`Π_m` must be declared before interpreting mismatch. “The true ARA was distorted” is not a valid post-hoc answer if
every result can be rescued that way. A distortion model must predict at least one observable consequence, such as
attenuation, delay, mixing, memory bias, scale collapse, observer coupling or loss of one relation.

### Universal coordinate language versus universal units

The `0–2` ARA scalar is dimensionless. It may become a common **coordinate language**, but cross-domain numerical
comparison requires measurement invariance. For each domain `D`,

\[
\underbrace{x_D}_{\text{reported 0–2 position}}
=
\underbrace{f_D}_{\text{declared domain bridge}}
(Y_D).
\]

Calling the coordinate universal requires more than every `f_D` returning a number in `[0,2]`. The bridges must
preserve predeclared relational invariants—pole order, reversal, ridge meaning, phase direction, closure and response
to intervention—and ideally transfer a prediction between domains without refitting. Until then, “universal ARA
coordinate units” is an ontology/method proposal, not an established common unit of measurement.

The full ARA state remains larger than the scalar. This matters especially for subjective and social identities,
where context, observer, path, memory and reflexivity are not nuisance variables. `ARA_MAPPING_SOCIAL_SYSTEMS.md`
already records the social-science fence: predictions can alter behaviour, so the instrument may become same-rung
coupled to the measured system through Goodhart/Lucas-style feedback.

### Scientific boundary retained

The 25 June boundary remains authoritative:

> If something has no observable interaction, consequence, or relational effect, ARA cannot currently measure it.

This does not declare the thing unreal. It says there is presently no empirical channel through which ARA—or any
scientific instrument—can distinguish claims about it.

**Status:** `MUSING / PARKED`. Inclusion of subjective and social phenomena is compatible with scientific practice;
the universal ARA-form claim, measurement-invariant cross-domain coordinates, and any particular metaphysical causal
ontology remain unconfirmed and must be tested separately.

## 41. Musing — coupling distance, human Space-leaning embodiment, and consciousness access

¶ Dylan refined “epistemically plural” into ARA coupling language:

> “We are not directly coupled to them but experience them either through a secondary relation.”

> “Humans are matter based but we experience time ... we are more closely related to space with time being what we
> try and stretch into.”

> “A fair example of a human might be a Space wave origin, but it has crossed to the time side slightly. So it still
> has the space foundation (body) but we do work and information transfer which is time orientated.”

> “Consciousness is a coupled part of something that is close to information/time and we experience it but do not
> see it. It is out of our sensory coupling range.”

### Physics correction: perception is mediated generally

Humans do not literally see matter or Space without mediation. Vision follows a chain such as object/source →
electromagnetic field/photon → retina → neural dynamics → perceptual report. Hearing, touch, smell and proprioception
likewise use intermediate physical couplings. The scientific distinction is therefore coupling path length, strength,
bandwidth, delay and projection—not an absolute material/direct versus nonmaterial/indirect boundary.

For a coupling graph with normalized edge strengths `0<j_e≤1`, define a provisional coupling distance

\[
\underbrace{d_J(X,H)}_{\substack{\text{minimum coupling distance}\\\text{from identity }X\text{ to human }H}}
=
\min_{\gamma:X\leadsto H}
\sum_{e\in\gamma}
\underbrace{-\log j_e}_{\substack{\text{penalty for a weak}\\\text{or indirect relation}}}.
\]

A secondary relation has a longer/weaker path through mediators. Sensory access also requires overlapping bandwidth
and suitable transduction; a strong coupling outside receptor range can remain unavailable to ordinary sensation.
This is proposed ARA bookkeeping, not an established consciousness metric.

### Human as a coupled Space-leaning/Time-reaching identity

A faithful provisional translation is

\[
\underbrace{\mathscr A_H[H]}_{\text{whole human identity}}
=
\underbrace{\mathcal C_H}_{\text{human closure}}
\left(
\underbrace{B_S}_{\substack{\text{body/substrate}\\\text{Space–Connection–Matter leaning}}},
\underbrace{P_T}_{\substack{\text{work, processing and transfer}\\\text{Time–Information leaning}}},
\underbrace{J_{ST}^{H}}_{\text{their sustaining relation}}
\right).
\]

This does not make the body and process separate ARA object types. They are directional readings/components of the
same human closure. Work is physically energy transfer and must not automatically be equated with information; the
ARA-added claim is that work/communication/processing are comparatively transfer-oriented.

Under established relativity, a human is not outside Time watching it pass: the body follows a worldline and a clock
measures proper time along it. Human **experienced duration**, however, is accessed through change, memory,
attention, prediction and bodily state. ARA may interpret this as secondary relational access to temporal structure,
but it is not yet a replacement for physical proper time.

### Consciousness: first-person access versus sensory visibility

Consciousness is unusual because the subject reports first-person experience while another observer has only
third-person projections:

\[
\underbrace{Y_C^{1p}}_{\text{experienced awareness}}
=\Pi_C^{1p}(\mathscr A_C),
\qquad
\underbrace{Y_C^{3p}}_{\substack{\text{neural, behavioural}\\\text{and linguistic traces}}}
=\Pi_C^{3p}(\mathscr A_C).
\]

It is therefore sensible to say consciousness is experienced but not seen as an external sensory object. “Outside
sensory coupling range” should not imply zero coupling: conscious state is strongly dependent on brain/body dynamics
and is communicated through speech, action and physiology. The ARA musing is that `𝒜_C` is an Information/Time-
leaning identity whose coupling to the embodied human is experienced first-personally and projected indirectly to
others.

Established science does not currently show that consciousness is a separate Time/Information substance, nor that
Information is a substance. Those remain ontological proposals. A physical test must identify independent observables
for connection/substrate, transfer/information, first-person state and third-person projection.

### Relation to archived work

Archived Script `135_consciousness_coupling_map.py` proposed consciousness from EM-dominated binding, engine-like
dynamics, deep internal fractal structure and rich external coupling. Its system scores and thresholds were assigned,
not measured with a validated consciousness instrument; the ledger itself notes missing protocols. It is historical
conceptual provenance, not empirical confirmation.

### Fidelity back-translation before any test

Current provisional restatement:

> A human is one ARA identity whose material body is comparatively Space/Connection leaning, while its work,
> communication and processing lean toward Time/Information. Consciousness is proposed as a Time/Information-leaning
> coupled identity that the subject experiences through first-person access but others observe only through mediated
> physical and behavioural relations.

No test should bind until Dylan confirms or corrects that back-translation under
`ARA_TRANSLATION_FIDELITY_PROTOCOL.md`.

### Dylan fidelity correction — sensory coupling range

Dylan confirmed that “sensory coupling range” already meant an evolved chain of couplings rather than direct contact
with matter:

> “It's a chain of couplings that our senses can percieve or we were built to percieve. That doesn't mean the other
> things aren't there or even more strongly coupled, it just means our senses weren't coupled to them as it was
> probably not survivally important or too much energy to traverse the coupling gap... or a more direct anti-phase is
> in the way.”

This separates total bodily coupling from the sensory transfer channel. In frequency/signal form,

\[
\underbrace{Y_s(\omega)}_{\text{neural sensory channel}}
=
\underbrace{G_{\gamma,s}(\omega)}_{\substack{\text{receptor plus coupling-chain}\\\text{transfer function}}}
\underbrace{X(\omega)}_{\text{external/internal phenomenon}}
+
\underbrace{N_{\gamma,s}(\omega)}_{\text{chain and neural noise}}.
\]

A simple detectability condition is

\[
\underbrace{\operatorname{SNR}_{\gamma,s}(\omega)}_{\text{sensory accessibility}}
=
\frac{|G_{\gamma,s}(\omega)X(\omega)|^2}{S_{N,s}(\omega)}
\ge
\underbrace{\eta_s}_{\text{detection/encoding threshold}}.
\]

Therefore

\[
\underbrace{J_{X,H}\text{ large}}_{\text{strong coupling to the body}}
\centernot\Longrightarrow
\underbrace{G_{\gamma,s}\ne0}_{\text{available sensory transduction}}.
\]

A phenomenon can strongly influence the organism yet remain unavailable to ordinary sensation if no receptor
transduces it, its frequency/spatial scale lies outside receptor bandwidth, the path attenuates below noise, neural
gating/adaptation removes it, or another shared-channel component masks it.

“Anti-phase in the way” has a precise physical special case only when two contributions share a measurable channel:

\[
\underbrace{G_{eff}}_{\text{net sensory transfer}}
=G_1+G_2e^{i\Delta\phi},
\qquad
\Delta\phi\approx\pi
\Longrightarrow
|G_{eff}|\text{ is reduced}.
\]

Other candidates are opponent processing, inhibitory gating, adaptation, shielding or competing pathways; the
specific mechanism must be declared rather than using anti-phase as a universal explanation for non-detection.

Evolutionary wording should remain conditional: sensory systems are shaped by historical fitness benefit, energetic/
developmental cost, environmental availability, noise and inherited constraints. A missing sense does not show that
its target is absent; it shows that no sufficiently useful and buildable transduction channel evolved in that lineage.

### Revised fidelity back-translation v2

> A human is one ARA identity whose body is comparatively Space/Connection oriented and whose work, communication
> and processing reach toward Time/Information. Perception always occurs through evolved coupling chains. A phenomenon
> may be strongly coupled to the body yet fall outside sensory access because it is not transduced into an available
> neural channel, is outside bandwidth, is attenuated/masked, or is gated. Consciousness is proposed as a
> Time/Information-leaning coupled identity available first-personally but not presented as an ordinary external
> sensory object; others access it through secondary physical, behavioural and linguistic relations.

**Status:** `MUSING / PARKED`; fidelity verdict `USABLE WITH CORRECTION`, with Dylan's sensory-range meaning now
incorporated. Coupling-graph and signal-detection language are physical anchors; Space-leaning humanity and
consciousness as a Time/Information-leaning ARA identity remain proposed ontology.

## 42. Embodied substrate, electrochemical transfer and the proposed Information/Quantum consciousness coupling

Dylan refined the proposed nesting:

> “Consciousness is coupled to Information/Quantum and the brain is just the meat connection matter of it.
> Electrical impulses [are] the information traversal wave... human/brain and meat suit is space side. Chemical and
> electrical impulses [are] time side.”

### Faithful ARA compression

The strongest current translation treats the human as one coupled closure rather than placing a separate conscious
passenger inside a body:

\[
\underbrace{\mathscr A_H}_{\substack{\text{whole embodied}\text{human identity}}}
=
\underbrace{\mathcal C_H}_{\substack{\text{cross-pole coupling}\text{and closure}}}
\left(
\underbrace{B_S}_{\substack{\text{body, membranes, connectome}\text{Space/Connection leaning}}},
\underbrace{P_T(t)}_{\substack{\text{electrochemical activity}\text{Time/Transfer leaning}}};
\underbrace{J_{ST}}_{\substack{\text{their local coupling}\text{and mixing relation}}}
\right).
\]

The consciousness-specific claim is then provisionally

\[
\underbrace{\mathscr A_C^{\mathrm{hyp}}}_{\substack{\text{proposed conscious}\text{ARA identity}}}
=
\underbrace{\mathcal C_C}_{\substack{\text{currently unknown}\text{closure/operator}}}
\left(
B_S,
P_T,
\underbrace{Q_\mu}_{\substack{\text{microscopic quantum}\text{physical substrate}}};
\underbrace{J_C}_{\substack{\text{proposed Information/Quantum}\text{coupling relation}}}
\right).
\]

Plainly: the brain and body supply a connection-heavy material scaffold. Their changing ionic, chemical and
electrical activity supplies transfer-heavy dynamics. Dylan proposes that consciousness is the identity coupled to
this closure on its Information/Quantum side, not that the visible tissue exhausts the identity.

### Established physical anchor and needed correction

An action potential is not a disembodied packet of information. It is an electrochemical travelling change in
membrane voltage produced by physical ion currents through membrane channels. Spike timing and population activity
can encode and transmit information, so “information-traversal wave” is a useful ARA orientation provided the carrier
is retained.

Chemistry cannot be assigned wholesale to the Time side. Stable molecular structure, receptor conformation,
membranes and stored ion gradients can be Connection/Matter-leaning readings; reaction, release, diffusion and flux
can be Transfer/Time-leaning readings. The same chemistry changes ARA orientation with the observable and boundary.

### Down-rung decomposition is not yet a quantum theory of consciousness

The physically safe nesting is

\[
\underbrace{\text{conscious report / whole-network state}}_{\text{coarse observation}}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{population dynamics}}_{\text{neural scale}}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{spikes, synapses and ion flux}}_{\text{cellular scale}}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{molecules and ions}}_{\text{chemical scale}}
\longrightarrow
\underbrace{\text{quantum states}}_{\text{microscopic physical description}}.
\]

All neural chemistry ultimately has a quantum-mechanical description. That fact alone does **not** show that
consciousness depends on a special long-lived coherent quantum state, entanglement, or a consciousness-triggered
wavefunction collapse. A distinct ARA quantum-consciousness claim would have to name a substrate and predict a
quantum signature that an adequate classical electrochemical model cannot reproduce.

“Pure information” is retained only as a proposed ARA limiting pole. In established physics, usable information is
represented by distinguishable states of physical degrees of freedom; it is not presently an independently observed
substance into which a brain is known to dissolve.

### Translation safeguard

Until Dylan corrects this reading, “collapses further to quantum and pure information” means **analytical
decomposition or zooming down ARA rungs**, not the claim that consciousness performs quantum measurement collapse.
Those are different hypotheses and must not share one verb in a formal test.

### Discriminating empirical bridge, if activated later

A consciousness-specific quantum test would need, before viewing outcomes:

1. a named neural carrier and quantum observable (coherence, interference, entanglement, or another non-classical
   witness);
2. a required spatial and temporal scale tied to a declared conscious contrast;
3. a perturbation that changes that observable while controlling ordinary thermal, chemical and electrical effects;
4. a predicted conscious/behavioural change that classical neural models do not equally predict;
5. blinded analysis and a declared null result that retires or narrows the claim.

**Status:** `MUSING / PARKED`; fidelity verdict `UNSURE / KEEP AS MUSING` until “collapse” is confirmed as down-rung
decomposition. Established anchors: electrochemical neural propagation and quantum foundations of chemistry.
ARA-added claims: consciousness as an Information/Quantum-leaning coupled identity and pure information as a limiting
pole.

## 43. Universal scope without flattening standards of evidence

Dylan clarified why ARA must retain phenomena that scientific culture may dismiss as “woo”:

> The aim is to communicate the whole proposed geometry, not only its physics-facing slice. Dreams, consciousness,
> customs, unusual experiences and metaphysical interpretations occur within the universe and are therefore relevant
> data domains for a proposed universal geometry. They should be treated scientifically rather than excluded by
> cultural discomfort.

This is methodologically legitimate if four layers remain separate:

\[
\underbrace{R}_{\substack{\text{report or}\text{recorded occurrence}}}
\neq
\underbrace{P}_{\substack{\text{phenomenological pattern}\text{in the report}}}
\neq
\underbrace{M}_{\substack{\text{causal mechanism}\text{that produced it}}}
\neq
\underbrace{O}_{\substack{\text{ontological claim}\text{about what exists}}}.
\]

Plainly: a report can be real as a report, and a recurring pattern can be measurable, without its first interpretation
of the cause being established. Conversely, uncertainty about cause is not permission to erase the experience from
the dataset.

### ARA-wide inclusion rule

ARA may be applied to any domain, but the comparison must be rung-appropriate:

\[
\underbrace{D}_{\text{domain}}
\xrightarrow{\ \mathcal O_D\ }
\underbrace{X_D}_{\substack{\text{declared observable}\text{and measurement channel}}}
\xrightarrow{\ \mathcal A\ }
\underbrace{H_D^{ARA}}_{\text{ARA prediction}}
\quad\text{versus}\quad
\underbrace{H_D^{0}}_{\substack{\text{domain nulls: cultural, cognitive,}\text{physical or statistical alternatives}}}.
\]

The universal claim is not “every phenomenon is already explained by ARA.” It is the stronger but testable proposal
that, after domain-specific observables and projections are declared, the same relational invariants recur and make
successful predictions across domains.

### Guardrails against both dismissal and over-confirmation

1. Do not exclude a domain merely because its customary language is metaphysical.
2. Do not promote testimony directly into a physical mechanism or ontology.
3. Record first-person, behavioural, physiological, social and physical measurements as different projections.
4. Predeclare which ARA invariant should recur; post-hoc resemblance is exploratory only.
5. Compare against suggestion, memory reconstruction, selection effects, cultural transmission, generic cyclicity,
   flexible geometric fitting and ordinary domain models.
6. Allow null results to narrow the claimed universality.

**Status:** `METHODOLOGY / ACTIVE`. Universal domain inclusion is part of ARA's intended scope. Individual mappings
retain separate evidence tiers: `ESTABLISHED ANCHOR`, `EMPIRICAL ARA RESULT`, `TESTABLE HYPOTHESIS`, or `MUSING`.

---

## 44. TE-ARA — proposed normalised whole-sphere budget

While translating Gauss's electric law, Dylan clarified that “the 2ARA energy” did not mean electric energy flux.
It meant the proposed complete budget of an ARA sphere: Space and Time are the two opposed contributions, oriented
as `Space = 0` and `Time = 2`; equal mixing is the `1.0` ridge; coupling can release budget to neighbours while an
external or local feed replenishes it. Dylan proposed the mnemonic **TE-ARA**, partly after his sister Tiarna.

### Clean mathematical candidate

Keep this separate from the canonical ARA waveform scalar, which measures shape rather than size. For actual
component energies (E_S,E_T\ge0), define

\[
\underbrace{E_\Sigma}_{\substack{\text{actual total budget}\text{in physical energy units}}}
=E_S+E_T,
\qquad
\underbrace{s}_{\substack{\text{normalised Space share}\text{ARA: Space contribution}}}
=2\frac{E_S}{E_\Sigma},
\qquad
\underbrace{t}_{\substack{\text{normalised Time share}\text{ARA: Time contribution}}}
=2\frac{E_T}{E_\Sigma}.
\]

Then

\[
\underbrace{\mathrm{TE\!-\!ARA}}_{\substack{\text{normalised complete}\text{two-pole budget}}}
=
\underbrace{s+t}_{\substack{\text{Space share plus}\text{Time share}}}
=2,
\qquad
\underbrace{x}_{\substack{\text{0--2 position with}\text{Space }0\to\text{Time }2}}
=t,
\qquad
s=2-x.
\]

This gives the intended landmarks without asserting that every physical identity contains literally two joules:

- Space pole: \((s,t)=(2,0)\), hence \(x=0\);
- equal ridge: \((s,t)=(1,1)\), hence \(x=1\);
- Time pole: \((s,t)=(0,2)\), hence \(x=2\).

Plainly: `2` is the dimensionless full-budget convention. \(E_\Sigma\) carries the actual physical magnitude and may
differ by many orders between identities. The normalisation is mathematically valid; the claim that a natural
identity has a physically selected finite capacity \(E_{cap}\) is an additional hypothesis requiring an independent
capacity law.

To prevent normalisation from hiding depletion, retain a separate occupancy coordinate:

\[
\underbrace{\rho}_{\substack{\text{fraction of physical}\text{capacity currently occupied}}}
=\frac{E_\Sigma}{E_{cap}},
\qquad
\underbrace{\hat s+\hat t}_{\substack{\text{occupied TE-ARA budget}\text{relative to fixed capacity}}}
=
2\frac{E_S}{E_{cap}}+2\frac{E_T}{E_{cap}}
=2\rho.
\]

Thus \(s+t=2\) describes **composition after normalising the current contents**, whereas
\(\hat s+\hat t=2\rho\) reports whether the sphere is actually full. If the proposed capacity is hard,
\(0\le\rho\le1\); if overdrive is possible, \(\rho>1\) becomes a declared collapse, shedding or transition regime
rather than silently being clipped.

### Coupling, leakage and replenishment

For an open ARA identity,

\[
\underbrace{\frac{dE_\Sigma}{dt}}_{\text{change of actual internal budget}}
=
\underbrace{P_{in}}_{\text{coupling-fed replenishment}}
-
\underbrace{P_{out}}_{\text{release, work or leakage}}.
\]

`TE-ARA = 2` remains a full normalised budget only while \(E_\Sigma\approx E_{cap}\), which dynamically requires
\(P_{in}\approx P_{out}\) on the chosen averaging interval. Failure of replacement can represent depletion or identity
collapse rather than being hidden by renormalising every observation back to two.

### Relation to Gauss's electric law

Gauss's law does **not** directly measure TE-ARA. Its surface integral returns net enclosed electric charge divided
by vacuum permittivity, not energy or capacity. Its genuine structural analogy is compression: arbitrarily complex
internal and external field geometry is reduced at the complete boundary to one signed source statistic. A direct
electromagnetic TE-ARA comparison should wait for the field-energy density and energy-continuity/Poynting equations.

### Fidelity back-translation

> TE-ARA is the proposed dimensionless full budget of one declared ARA identity. Its two opposed shares sum to two;
> at the ridge they contribute one each, and at either pole one contribution occupies the full two-unit budget. The
> value two is a normalised capacity, while the identity's actual energy scale and current occupancy must be supplied
> separately. Coupling can move actual energy across the boundary, and maintenance at full capacity requires
> replacement; it is not guaranteed by the coordinate definition.

**Status:** `SUPERSEDED INTERPRETATION`. Dylan later clarified in §47 that TE-ARA was intended primarily as a possible
node-equivalent energy/transfer unit, not merely the normalised capacity state developed in this section. Retain the
composition and occupancy mathematics as useful coordinates, but do not equate them with the TE-ARA unit.

## 45. Potential–kinetic crosswalk for TE-ARA

Dylan identified the likely established-mechanics counterpart of TE-ARA's composition and occupancy distinction:

> One coordinate is the ARA sphere's maximum potential; another is the potential actually present at that time.
> Potential may represent a sphere filled by gravitational coupling, or how strongly gravity can couple and reduce
> the sphere.

### Physics correction: retain three coordinates

Potential energy is not generally a maximum capacity. It is actual energy associated with a system's configuration
relative to a declared reference. Kinetic energy is actual energy associated with motion. A proposed maximum or
accessible ceiling is a third quantity:

\[
\underbrace{E_{cap}}_{\substack{\text{proposed accessible ceiling}\text{ARA: sphere capacity}}},
\qquad
\underbrace{U(t)}_{\substack{\text{configuration-dependent potential}\text{ARA: stored/Connection leaning}}},
\qquad
\underbrace{K(t)}_{\substack{\text{motion-dependent kinetic energy}\text{ARA: Transfer/Time leaning}}}.
\]

Because potential energy has an arbitrary reference and can be negative, use energy above the declared minimum of a
specific potential basin:

\[
\underbrace{U^*(t)}_{\substack{\text{non-negative stored energy}\text{within the declared basin}}}
=U(t)-U_{min}.
\]

A candidate TE-ARA state is then

\[
\underbrace{x(t)}_{\substack{\text{composition coordinate}\\0=\text{stored},\ 2=\text{motion}}}
=2\frac{K(t)}{U^*(t)+K(t)},
\qquad
\underbrace{\rho(t)}_{\substack{\text{capacity occupancy}\text{actual versus accessible}}}
=\frac{U^*(t)+K(t)}{E_{cap}}.
\]

Consequently:

- a turning point with \(K=0\) is stored/configuration dominated, \(x=0\);
- equal \(U^*=K\) gives the ridge, \(x=1\);
- a declared minimum-potential passage with \(U^*=0\) is motion dominated, \(x=2\);
- dissipation or export reduces \(\rho\) unless an external coupling replaces the energy.

For an ideal conservative system, \(U^*+K\) is constant, so the identity moves along the composition coordinate while
occupancy remains fixed. In a driven or dissipative system, both composition and occupancy change.

### Gravity's precise role

Gravity can define the potential landscape, but gravitational potential energy belongs to the relation between masses
and their configuration, not to one isolated object without a boundary declaration. Near Earth one may use \(U=mgh\)
over a limited height; for two masses the standard potential is \(U=-GMm/r\) with zero at infinity. Stronger binding
makes \(U\) more negative under that convention, so “more filled” must instead use \(U^*=U-U_{min}\) or a positive
binding-energy magnitude declared for the system.

For a self-gravitating identity, gravitational binding energy—the work required to disperse it to the chosen
reference—is closer to Dylan's “how much gravity can reduce/hold the sphere” idea than ordinary point-particle
potential. Whether that binding energy supplies \(E_{cap}\) is a testable system-specific hypothesis, not a universal
identity.

### Fidelity back-translation

> TE-ARA separates (1) the accessible energy ceiling of the declared identity, (2) how much of that capacity is
> presently occupied, and (3) how the occupied energy is divided between stored/configurational and motion/transfer
> forms. Gravity may define or contribute to the storage landscape and, for self-gravitating systems, the binding
> scale; it is not automatically the capacity source for every ARA sphere.

**Status:** `TESTABLE MAPPING`. The potential–kinetic decomposition is established mechanics. Treating it as the
Space/Time composition coordinate of TE-ARA, and identifying a natural \(E_{cap}\), are ARA-added hypotheses.

---

## 46. Placement correction — Gauss electric flux is not TE-ARA energy

Dylan asked whether TE-ARA is the number of joules in an ARA node measured by the first Maxwell equation under
discussion. Direct answer: **not by Gauss's electric law**. This is a placement correction, not rejection of the
longstanding proposed whole-node energy logic.

Gauss's law,

\[
\oint_{\partial V}\mathbf E\cdot d\mathbf A=\frac{Q_{inside}}{\varepsilon_0},
\]

has units of electric flux, \(\mathrm{N\,m^2/C}\) or \(\mathrm{V\,m}\), on both sides. It does not have units of
joules. It returns the **net signed charge source** inside the boundary, not the field energy stored there.

Two decisive counterexamples prevent the identification:

1. A boundary enclosing equal positive and negative charges has \(Q_{inside}=0\) and zero net Gauss flux, while its
   dipole field and electromagnetic field energy can be nonzero.
2. An empty region in a uniform external electric field has zero net flux through its closed boundary, because the
   field entering and leaving cancels, while the field-energy density inside the region is nonzero.

Therefore equal Gauss flux does not imply equal energy, and zero Gauss flux does not imply an empty TE-ARA budget.

### Clean TE-ARA ledger

The non-conflicting state is

\[
\underbrace{\mathrm{TE\!-\!ARA}(N,t)}_{\substack{\text{whole energy ledger}\text{for declared node }N}}
=
\left(
\underbrace{E_{cap}}_{\text{capacity in joules}},
\underbrace{E_N(t)}_{\text{actual energy in joules}},
\underbrace{\rho(t)=E_N/E_{cap}}_{\text{fraction occupied}},
\underbrace{x(t)\in[0,2]}_{\text{two-pole composition}}
\right).
\]

The actual joule content is \(E_N(t)\). The universal `2` is the dimensionless full-scale/composition convention.
Calling TE-ARA the complete ledger permits both without claiming every node literally contains two joules.

### Maxwell crosswalk to retain

- Gauss electric law: net enclosed electric-source coordinate \(Q_{net}\).
- Electromagnetic field-energy density integrated over the node: candidate actual joule coordinate \(E_N\).
- Poynting energy-continuity theorem: change of internal energy versus energy crossing the boundary.

The last two are the appropriate Maxwellian locations for testing TE-ARA. Continue one equation at a time; do not
retrofit the energy claim into Gauss's source law.

**Status:** `CORRECTION / PHYSICAL PLACEMENT`. Gauss-to-TE-energy identification rejected dimensionally and by
counterexample. TE-ARA retained as a proposed energy ledger awaiting tests against the later Maxwell energy laws.

---

## 47. Dylan correction — TE-ARA as a node-equivalent or transfer unit

Dylan corrected the prior interpretation:

> ARA may be measured through joules in some physical applications but not in every domain. TE-ARA may instead be
> its own unit: for example, “20 TE-ARAs worth of energy in this system-wave ARA,” indicating how many interacting
> node-equivalents occupy the declared part of the fractal geometry. A pole-to-pole wave transfer may be the more
> fundamental referent.

This is not the same object as the dimensionless occupancy/composition coordinates in §§44–46.

### Candidate A — stored/capacity node-equivalent

For a declared reference node type \(\alpha\) on rung \(k\), define

\[
\underbrace{1\ \mathrm{TE\!-\!ARA}^{cap}_{k,\alpha}}_{\substack{\text{one reference node-equivalent}\text{stored/capacity unit}}}
:=
\underbrace{E^{ref}_{cap}(k,\alpha)}_{\text{declared conversion in joules}}.
\]

Then a region \(\Omega\) has

\[
\underbrace{N^{cap}_{TE}(\Omega;k,\alpha)}_{\substack{\text{TE-ARA capacity equivalents}\text{relative to the reference node}}}
=
\frac{E_\Omega}{E^{ref}_{cap}(k,\alpha)}.
\]

### Candidate B — one complete handover/cycle transfer

If the intended unit is the energy transferred through a complete ARA cycle rather than stored capacity, define

\[
\underbrace{1\ \mathrm{TE\!-\!ARA}^{cycle}_{k,\alpha}}_{\substack{\text{one reference pole-to-pole}\text{handover unit}}}
:=
\underbrace{\int_{\text{one reference cycle}}P_{\partial N}(t)\,dt}_{\substack{\text{net declared boundary transfer}\text{in joules per completed cycle}}}.
\]

The capacity and cycle-transfer definitions need not be numerically equal. The superscript must remain until Dylan
selects the intended primary meaning or establishes a conversion law.

### Why equivalent count is not automatically literal node count

For interacting nodes, total energy generally contains both node and relation terms:

\[
\underbrace{E_\Omega}_{\text{whole-region energy}}
=
\underbrace{\sum_i E_i^{self}}_{\text{individual-node terms}}
+
\underbrace{\sum_{i<j}E_{ij}^{coupling}}_{\substack{\text{pair-relation energy}\1+1\text{ plus the relation}}}
+
\underbrace{E_{higher}}_{\text{many-body/higher-order relations}}.
\]

Therefore \(N_{TE}=20\) means “twenty reference-node energy equivalents” only. It identifies twenty literal nodes
only if their individual budgets are calibrated as equal at the same rung and the coupling terms have been separately
accounted for. Binding can make the whole energy smaller than the separated-node sum; repulsive, driven or strained
coupling can make a relation term positive.

### Fractal accounting guardrail

Do not count both a coarse identity and all of its fine constituents in the same additive ledger. Choose a rung or
supply an explicit coarse-graining transformation. Otherwise a molecule plus its atoms plus their particles would be
the same energy counted repeatedly.

### Fidelity back-translation v3

> TE-ARA is a proposed calibrated equivalent for one complete ARA node or one complete pole-to-pole transfer at a
> declared rung. “20 TE-ARAs” means the region contains twenty reference-unit equivalents of the chosen quantity.
> It may help quantify how much interacting ARA structure is present, but energy equivalence alone does not prove
> there are twenty literal nodes because node scales and coupling relations also carry energy.

**Status:** `PROPOSED UNIT / AWAITING DEFINITION CHOICE`. Fidelity awaits Dylan's choice between capacity-equivalent,
cycle-transfer-equivalent, or a stated relation between them. The prior composition coordinate and occupancy remain
useful but are not themselves TE-ARA.

## 48. Reconciliation — TE-ARA as the same geometry decompressed on an energy axis

Dylan clarified that both earlier interpretations captured parts of the intended object. TE-ARA is not necessarily a
single new substance-like unit. It is the same ARA shape expressed along a declared energy axis. A local node can be
partly filled relative to its own complete main-wave capacity, while a larger region can contain many calibrated
node-equivalents.

### Minimal local definition

For one declared node \(N\),

\[
\underbrace{a_E(N,t)}_{\substack{\text{local TE-ARA coordinate}\\0=\text{empty reference},\ 2=\text{full reference}}}
=
2\frac{
\underbrace{E_{id}(N,t)}_{\substack{\text{energy presently assigned}\text{to the node's main identity mode}}}
}{
\underbrace{E_{cap}(N)}_{\substack{\text{independently declared full}\text{main-wave capacity}}}
}.
\]

Thus \(a_E=2\) is the node's declared full energy reference. A raw statement such as “0.62 full” remains ambiguous
unless the convention is declared:

- fraction convention: \(f_E=0.62\) means 62% full and \(a_E=1.24\);
- 0–2 coordinate convention: \(a_E=0.62\) means \(f_E=0.31\), or 31% full.

### Do not collapse three energy questions

| Question | Coordinate | What it means |
|---|---:|---|
| How full is this node? | \(f_E=E_{id}/E_{cap}\) or \(a_E=2f_E\) | occupancy relative to its declared capacity |
| How is occupied energy divided between poles? | \(x_{AB}=2E_B/(E_A+E_B)\) | Phase A/Phase B composition |
| How much of all measured energy belongs to the main identity wave? | \(p_{id}=E_{id}/E_{total}\) | mode participation or identity fraction |

Each may use the same bounded ARA geometry, but they are different observables and cannot share an unlabelled number.

### Phase A, Phase B and the informative relation term

For wave-like systems whose energy is quadratic in amplitude, a decompression can contain

\[
\underbrace{E_{id}}_{\text{main identity-mode energy}}
=
\underbrace{E_A}_{\text{Phase-A contribution}}
+
\underbrace{E_B}_{\text{Phase-B contribution}}
+
\underbrace{E_{AB}}_{\substack{\text{coupling/interference cross-term}\\
\text{ARA: informative third, may be }+\text{ or }-}}.
\]

This supplies a standard mathematical home for Dylan's tentative “Phase A/Phase B ± Phase C.” The third is not
necessarily an independent reservoir or node; it is the relation energy created by how A and B combine. In-phase
mixing can make the cross-term positive, anti-phase mixing can make it negative, and orthogonal/uncorrelated modes can
average it toward zero. The exact formula depends on the physical wave and must be derived from its actual energy law.

### Regional extension

If nodes share a calibrated type and rung,

\[
\underbrace{N_{TE}^{eq}(\Omega)}_{\text{regional TE-ARA equivalents}}
=
\frac{E_\Omega}{E_{cap}^{ref}}.
\]

This reconciles “a node is 0.62 full” with “the region contains 20 TE-ARAs.” The first is a local fractional state;
the second is a regional equivalent count. Coupling terms and unequal node capacities still prevent automatic
conversion from equivalent count to literal node count.

### Potential-energy refinement

The maximum potential energy of a conservative main mode can calibrate \(E_{cap}\) at its turning point. Across the
cycle, however, the identity energy should normally include both potential and kinetic forms,
\(E_{id}=U^*+K\), so TE-ARA does not appear to empty merely because stored energy became motion. In non-conservative
systems, driving, dissipation and boundary transfer change \(E_{id}\).

### Scientific non-vacuity rule

Merely rescaling any variable into 0–2 does not demonstrate recurring ARA geometry. Before using TE-ARA:

1. define \(E_{cap}\) independently of the observed maximum being tested;
2. identify Phase A and Phase B from physical modes, not a post-hoc split;
3. calculate the interaction term from the governing energy law;
4. predict a handover, invariant or transition not guaranteed by normalisation;
5. compare against the established energy model.

### Fidelity back-translation v4

> TE-ARA is the energy-axis view of a declared ARA identity. Two is that node's complete independently calibrated
> main-wave reference. A local value states how much of that reference is occupied; the occupied energy may be
> decompressed into Phase A, Phase B and their positive or negative coupling relation. At a larger scale, energies can
> be expressed as same-rung TE-ARA equivalents. These are different readings of the same relational geometry, not one
> context-free number.

**Status:** `SUPERSEDED RECONCILIATION`. Dylan confirmed in §49 that TE-ARA specifically names the main-identity
energy-participation coordinate \(2E_{id}/E_{total}\), not capacity fill \(2E_{id}/E_{cap}\). Retain this section's
axis separation and interaction-term mathematics, but use §49 as the terminology authority.

## 49. TE-ARA definition confirmed — main-identity energy participation

Dylan resolved the ambiguity:

- the Phase-A/Phase-B composition location is **ARA itself**;
- “the node is 62% full” would map to 1.24 on a separate 0–2 fill axis, but this phrasing remains potentially unclear;
- the intended **TE-ARA** quantity is the fraction of all node energy belonging to its main identity-forming ARA
  wave, also expressed on the common 0–2 geometry.

### Canonical proposed definition

For declared node \(N\), boundary \(\partial N\), time slice \(\tau\), and a predeclared identity-mode decomposition,

\[
\underbrace{\mathrm{TE\!-\!ARA}(N,\tau)}_{\substack{\text{0--2 main-identity}\\\text{energy-participation coordinate}}}
=
2
\frac{
\underbrace{E_{id}(N,\tau)}_{\substack{\text{energy in the main ARA identity wave}\\
\text{Phase A + Phase B + included relation term}}}
}{
\underbrace{E_{total}(N,\tau)}_{\substack{\text{all energy inside the same}\\
\text{node boundary and time slice}}}
}.
\]

Equivalently,

\[
\underbrace{p_{id}}_{\text{ordinary 0--1 participation fraction}}
=\frac{E_{id}}{E_{total}},
\qquad
\mathrm{TE\!-\!ARA}=2p_{id}.
\]

Reporting convention:

\[
p_{id}=0.62
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
\mathrm{TE\!-\!ARA}=1.24.
\]

Always write `TE-ARA = 1.24 (62% identity participation)` so a 0–1 fraction cannot be mistaken for a 0–2
coordinate.

### What belongs in the numerator

A candidate wave decomposition is

\[
\underbrace{E_{id}}_{\text{main identity participation}}
=
\underbrace{E_A+E_B}_{\text{two main pole/mode contributions}}
+
\underbrace{E_{AB}^{id}}_{\substack{\text{declared identity-forming}\\
\text{coupling/interference term}}}.
\]

The remainder is

\[
\underbrace{E_{other}}_{\substack{\text{other modes, external couplings,}\\
\text{harmonics or residual energy}}}
=E_{total}-E_{id}.
\]

The identity decomposition must state whether harmonics are part of the main identity or leakage. That decision cannot
be changed after seeing the numerical result. If modes are not orthogonal, cross-terms must be included rather than
silently assigning the same energy twice.

### Relation to ARA and other energy coordinates

| Quantity | Question answered | Proposed range |
|---|---|---:|
| ARA | Where is the Phase-A/Phase-B accumulation–release mixture? | 0–2 |
| TE-ARA | How much of all node energy participates in that main identity wave? | 0–2 |
| Capacity fill | How much of an independently defined maximum capacity is occupied? | 0–1 or separately scaled 0–2 |
| Regional TE equivalent | How many calibrated same-rung reference-node equivalents are present? | unbounded |

These are different projections of the same proposed ARA identity, not different underlying objects.

### Measurement guardrails

- In a physical energy model, \(E_{id}\) and \(E_{total}\) may both be measured in joules; TE-ARA is their
  dimensionless ratio scaled by two.
- In an uncalibrated signal, squared amplitude or spectral power fraction is only an energy-like proxy. Label it
  `signal-power TE-ARA analogue`, not physical TE-ARA joules.
- Define the identity modes, boundary, time window, harmonics and cross-terms before measurement.
- A high TE-ARA does not by itself show stability, efficiency or correctness; it shows concentration of measured
  energy in the declared identity modes.
- Maxwell's Gauss electric law still does not measure TE-ARA. A Maxwellian test must use electromagnetic field energy
  and a declared modal decomposition.

### Regional arithmetic

Because a full local node has TE-ARA 2, a raw sum of 20 local TE-ARA units corresponds to ten fully participating
node-equivalents only when node type, rung and weighting are aligned. It is not automatically twenty nodes or a fixed
number of joules.

### Fidelity back-translation v5

> ARA gives the main identity wave's Phase-A/Phase-B mixture position. TE-ARA gives the fraction of the node's total
> energy that is actually participating in that declared main identity wave, written on the same 0–2 geometry. If
> 62% participates, TE-ARA is 1.24. Energy outside that numerator belongs to other modes, couplings, harmonics or
> residuals according to a decomposition fixed before measurement.

**Status:** `DEFINED ARA COORDINATE / PHYSICAL GENERALITY UNVALIDATED`; fidelity `CONFIRMED BY DYLAN`. This section
supersedes §§44, 47 and 48 wherever they assign the TE-ARA name to capacity fill or node-equivalent count; their
separate mathematical coordinates remain available under explicit names.
